---
title: "Modules"
description: "Smart contract modules utilities and implementations"
---

ERC-7579 modules that can be installed on any compliant account. Validators authorize user operations and ERC-1271 signatures, while executors trigger actions through the account's `executeFromExecutor` function. Both are built on top of abstract signer modules that store per-account signer configuration.

- [`ERC7579ECDSA`](#ERC7579ECDSA), [`ERC7579P256`](#ERC7579P256), [`ERC7579WebAuthn`](#ERC7579WebAuthn): Single-signer modules for ECDSA, P-256 and WebAuthn keys.
- [`ERC7579Multisig`](#ERC7579Multisig), [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted): Threshold and weighted multisignature modules using ERC-7913 signers.
- [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential), [`ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential`](#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential): Multisignature modules that keep signers and thresholds encrypted using FHE.
- [`ERC7579SessionKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys): Scoped, time-limited signing authority with per-session policies.
- [`ERC7579Validator`](#ERC7579Validator): Abstract validator module, with implementations for each signer module.
- [`ERC7579Executor`](#ERC7579Executor): Abstract executor module, extended by [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor) for time-delayed execution.
- [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor): Time-delayed account recovery authorized by a weighted set of guardians.
- [`ERC7579NativeSpender`](#ERC7579NativeSpender), [`ERC7579ERC20Spender`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender): Session-key-driven spending limits for native and ERC-20 tokens.

## `ERC7579ECDSA` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579ECDSA.sol">

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579ECDSA.sol";
```

Implementation of an `IERC7579Module` that uses an ECDSA signer for signature
validation.

This module provides a base implementation for ECDSA signer validation that can be
attached to any function through the [`ERC7579ECDSA._validateECDSA`](#ERC7579ECDSA-_validateECDSA-address-bytes32-bytes-) internal function.

<a id="ERC7579ECDSA-signer-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">signer(address account) → address</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ECDSA-signer-address-">#</a>
</div>

<div className="px-4">

Returns the authorized signer for the specified account.

<a id="ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
The `data` parameter should contain the signer address (20 bytes minimum).

If the account doesn't already have a signer configured, the first 20 bytes
of `data` will be used as the authorized signer address.
See [`ERC7579ECDSA._extractSigner`](#ERC7579ECDSA-_extractSigner-bytes-) for more details about the encoding.

Requirements:

* The `data` must be at least 20 bytes. Reverts with [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSAInvalidSignerLength`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSAInvalidSignerLength--) if not.
* The `data` must contain a valid signer address. Reverts with [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSAInvalidSigner`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSAInvalidSigner-address-) if not.
* The `account` must have no signer installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSAAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSAAlreadyInstalled--) if not.

Emits a [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSASignerSet`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSASignerSet-address-address-) event if the signer is set.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ECDSA-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ECDSA-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up the module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Removes the authorized signer for the calling account.

If the account has a configured signer, it will be cleared. If no signer
is configured, this function will behave as a no-op.

Emits a [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSASignerSet`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSASignerSet-address-address-) event if the signer is cleared.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ECDSA-setSigner-address-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ECDSA-setSigner-address-address-">#</a>
</div>

<div className="px-4">

Sets the signer for the specified account.

Requirements:

* The `newSigner` must not be the zero address.
* The caller must be the specified account.

Emits a [`ERC7579ECDSA.ERC7579ECDSASignerSet`](#ERC7579ECDSA-ERC7579ECDSASignerSet-address-address-) event.

</div>
</div>

## `ERC7579Multisig` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579Multisig.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579Multisig.sol";
```

Implementation of an `IERC7579Module` that uses ERC-7913 signers for multisignature
validation.

This module provides a base implementation for multisignature validation that can be
attached to any function through the [`ERC7579Multisig._validateMultisig`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateMultisig-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---) internal function. The signers
are represented using the ERC-7913 format, which concatenates a verifier address and
a key: `verifier || key`.

A smart account with this module installed can require multiple signers to approve
operations before they are executed, such as requiring 3-of-5 guardians to approve
a social recovery operation.

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
See `IERC7579Module-onInstall`. The `initData` encodes `signers` and `threshold`.

See [`ERC7579Multisig._decodeMultisigInitData`](#ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-) for the encoding details.

<Callout>
An account can only call onInstall once. If called directly by the account,
the signers will be set to the provided data. Future installations will revert with
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* The `signers` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigInvalidInitData`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigInvalidInitData--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig._decodeMultisigInitData`](#ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---) and [`ERC7579Multisig._setThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-_setThreshold-address-uint64-) for signer configuration requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Clears all signers and resets the threshold.

See `IERC7579Module-onUninstall`.

<Callout type="warn">
This function has unbounded gas costs and may become uncallable if the set grows too large.
See `EnumerableSet-clear`.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-getSigners-address-uint64-uint64-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">getSigners(address account, uint64 start, uint64 end) → bytes[]</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-getSigners-address-uint64-uint64-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns a slice of the set of authorized signers for the specified account.

Using `start = 0` and `end = type(uint64).max` will return the entire set of signers.

<Callout type="warn">
Depending on the `start` and `end`, this operation can copy a large amount of data to memory, which
can be expensive. This is designed for view accessors queried without gas fees. Using it in state-changing
functions may become uncallable if the slice grows too large.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-isSigner-address-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">isSigner(address account, bytes signer) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-isSigner-address-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether the `signer` is an authorized signer for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">threshold(address account) → uint64</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns the minimum number of signers required to approve a multisignature operation
for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">addSigners(bytes[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds new signers to the authorized set for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself.

Requirements:

* Each of `newSigners` must be at least 20 bytes long.
* Each of `newSigners` must not be already authorized.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-removeSigners-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">removeSigners(bytes[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-removeSigners-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Removes signers from the authorized set for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself.

Requirements:

* Each of `oldSigners` must be authorized.
* After removal, the threshold must still be reachable.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-setThreshold-uint64-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setThreshold(uint64 newThreshold)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-setThreshold-uint64-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets the threshold for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself.

Requirements:

* The threshold must be reachable with the current number of signers.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, bytes[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds the `newSigners` to those allowed to sign on behalf of the account.

Requirements:

* Each of `newSigners` must be at least 20 bytes long. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigInvalidSigner`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigInvalidSigner-bytes-) if not.
* Each of `newSigners` must not be authorized. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyExists`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyExists-bytes-) if it already exists.

<Callout>
This function does not validate that signers are controlled or represent appropriate entities. Integrators
must ensure signers are properly validated before adding them. Problematic signers can compromise
the multisig's security or functionality. Examples include uncontrolled addresses (e.g., `address(0)`),
the account's own address (which may cause recursive validation loops), or contracts that may unintentionally
allow arbitrary validation (e.g. using the identity precompile at `address(0x04)`, which would return the
ERC-1271 magic value for any `isValidSignature` call).
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, bytes[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Removes the `oldSigners` from the authorized signers for the account.

Requirements:

* Each of `oldSigners` must be authorized. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner-bytes-) if not.
* The threshold must remain reachable after removal. See [`ERC7579Multisig._validateReachableThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateReachableThreshold-address-) for details.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_setThreshold-address-uint64-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setThreshold(address account, uint64 newThreshold)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_setThreshold-address-uint64-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets the signatures `threshold` required to approve a multisignature operation.

Requirements:

* The threshold must be greater than 0. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigZeroThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigZeroThreshold--) if not.
* The threshold must be reachable with the current number of signers. See [`ERC7579Multisig._validateReachableThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateReachableThreshold-address-) for details.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_validateReachableThreshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateReachableThreshold(address account)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_validateReachableThreshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates the current threshold is reachable with the number of `signers`.

Requirements:

* The number of signers must be >= the threshold. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigUnreachableThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigUnreachableThreshold-uint64-uint64-) if not.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_validateMultisig-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

</div>

<div className="px-4">

Validates the multisignature operation by checking the threshold and the signatures.

Where `signingSigners` are the authorized signers and `signatures` are their corresponding
signatures of the operation `hash`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateSignatures(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes[] signingSigners, bytes[] signatures) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates the signatures using the signers and their corresponding signatures.
Returns whether the signers are authorized, are not duplicated and the signatures
are valid for the given hash.

<Callout type="warn">
Sorting the signers by their `keccak256` hash will improve the gas
efficiency of this function.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_validateThreshold-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates that the number of signers meets the [`ERC7579Multisig.threshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-) requirement.
Assumes the signers were already validated. See [`ERC7579Multisig._validateSignatures`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---) for more details.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeMultisigInitData(bytes initData) → bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold_</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes multisig initialization data from calldata without memory allocation.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold)`

Requirements:

* The `initData` must be at least 96 bytes long to contain the minimum ABI-encoded structure.
* The signers array offset must be properly aligned and within bounds.
* The array length field must be accessible within the provided data.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579MultisigConfidential` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579MultisigConfidential.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579MultisigConfidential.sol";
```

Implementation of an `IERC7579Module` that provides confidential multisignature
validation.

This module provides a base implementation for multisignature validation that can be
attached to any function through the [`ERC7579Multisig._validateMultisig`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateMultisig-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---) internal function. Unlike
the standard [`ERC7579Multisig`](#ERC7579Multisig) module, this confidential version uses Fully Homomorphic
Encryption (FHE) to keep signer identities and the threshold value encrypted on-chain.
This provides privacy benefits by hiding the identity of authorized signers and the
number of signatures required to approve operations.

A smart account with this module installed can require multiple signers to approve
operations, such as requiring 3-of-5 guardians to approve a social recovery operation,
while keeping both the signer identities and threshold confidential.

Operations are identified by their hash, which represents arbitrary data for the application
to validate. Therefore, replay protection and other considersations about the operation must be
handled by the application.

To request disclosure of the multisignature validation result for a given operation, the
application must call `requestDiscloseOperation` to make the result publicly decryptable,
then call `discloseOperation` to verify the decryption proof and get the result.

<Callout type="warn">
Due to the non-deterministic nature of FHE encryption, encrypting the same plaintext
uint256 value multiple times produces different ciphertexts. This means the same underlying signer
can be added multiple times with different encrypted handles, and duplicate detection by
comparing encrypted handles is not possible. Consider duplicate signers may cause the
threshold to become unreachable since [`ERC7579Multisig._validateSignatures`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---bytes---) checks for duplicates.
</Callout>

<Callout>
This module supports both address-based signers (EOAs and ERC-1271 contracts) and
ERC-7913 signers (arbitrary keys with verifiers). Addresses are cast to uint256 and
encrypted, while ERC-7913 keys are hashed with `keccak256(verifier || key)` before
encryption. This hybrid approach allows addresses to be recognizable after decryption,
while ERC-7913 keys require an off-chain mapping from hash to preimage.
</Callout>

<Callout>
The threshold can be set to 0 given it can't be validated in runtime. The account
operators must rely on external validation to ensure the threshold is not 0, which allows
arbitrary execution.
</Callout>

<Callout>
A per-account configuration nonce (from `Nonces`) is incremented on every configuration
change ([`ERC7579Multisig.addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---), [`ERC7579Multisig.removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-removeSigners-bytes---), [`ERC7579Multisig.setThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-setThreshold-uint64-), [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-)). Disclosure state is scoped
to the nonce active at the time of [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.requestDiscloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---), so any configuration change
automatically invalidates outstanding disclosure entries and closes the stale-approval window.
</Callout>

<Callout type="warn">
When using ERC-1271 contract signers, signature validity can change over time (e.g. the
signer contract updates its owner). Because [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.requestDiscloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---) snapshots the validation
result, a later call to [`ERC7579ECDSAValidator._rawERC7579Validation`](#ERC7579ECDSAValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-) may accept a stale authorization even if the
ERC-1271 signer would now reject the signature. Prefer signers with non-revocable signatures
(e.g. EOAs), or call [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.dismissDisclosure`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-dismissDisclosure--) to invalidate outstanding disclosures when an
ERC-1271 signer's validity changes.
</Callout>

<Callout type="warn">
This module provides confidentiality of signer identities and threshold values, but
does not provide confidentiality of threshold relationships among participants. Once a valid
disclosure is made via [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.requestDiscloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---), any party that has observed valid signatures
for the operation hash (e.g. from mempool or calldata) can probe arbitrary signer subsets to
infer the threshold or weight distribution. To limit the exposure window, accounts can perform
a signer reset after sensitive operations: re-encrypting the signer set with fresh FHE handles
(and optionally updating the threshold) invalidates any oracle knowledge accumulated over the
previous handle set, since FHE encryption is non-deterministic and old handles are unlinkable
to new ones.
</Callout>

<div className="bg-secondary p-4 rounded-md mb-6">
<h3 style={{ marginTop: "0"}}>Functions</h3>
<div className="font-mono">

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
See `IERC7579Module-onInstall`. The `initData` encodes externally encrypted
`signerIds` and `threshold`. See [`ERC7579Multisig._decodeMultisigInitData`](#ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-) for the encoding details.

<Callout>
An account can only call `onInstall` once. If called directly by the account,
the signerIds will be set to the provided data. Future installations will revert with
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* The `signerIds` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig._decodeMultisigInitData`](#ERC7579Multisig-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig.addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---) for encrypted signers configuration requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Clears all signerIds and resets the threshold.

See `IERC7579Module-onUninstall`.

<Callout type="warn">
This function has unbounded gas costs and may become uncallable if the set grows too large.
See `EnumerableSet-clear`.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-getSigners-address-uint32-uint32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">getSigners(address account, uint32 start, uint32 end) → euint256[]</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-getSigners-address-uint32-uint32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns a slice of the set of authorized encrypted signers for the specified account.

The returned signers are encrypted uint256 values (euint256). Using `start = 0` and
`end = type(uint32).max` will return the entire set of signerIds.

<Callout type="warn">
Depending on the `start` and `end`, this operation can copy a large amount of data to memory, which
can be expensive. This is designed for view accessors queried without gas fees. Using it in state-changing
functions may become uncallable if the slice grows too large.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-getSignerCount-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">getSignerCount(address account) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-getSignerCount-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns the number of authorized encrypted signers for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-isSigner-address-euint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">isSigner(address account, euint256 signerId) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-isSigner-address-euint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether the encrypted `signerId` is an authorized signer for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-threshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">threshold(address account) → euint32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-threshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns the encrypted minimum number of signers required to approve a multisignature
operation for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestedDisclosure-address-address-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">requestedDisclosure(address relayer, address account, bytes32 hash) → ebool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestedDisclosure-address-address-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether the multisignature validation result for an operation (hash) is requested for
disclosure under the account's current configuration nonce.

<Callout>
Returns an uninitialized handle if the configuration has changed since the disclosure was requested.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-disclosed-address-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">disclosed(address account, bytes32 hash) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-disclosed-address-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether the multisignature validation result for an operation (hash) is disclosed
under the account's current configuration nonce.

<Callout>
Returns false if the configuration has changed since the operation was disclosed.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-addSigners-externalEuint256---bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">addSigners(externalEuint256[] newSignersIds, bytes inputProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-addSigners-externalEuint256---bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds new encrypted signerIds to the authorized set for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself. See [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---).

Increments the account's configuration nonce, invalidating any outstanding disclosure entries
made under the previous nonce.

The signerIds are provided as externally encrypted uint256 values. Each signerId represents
either an address (cast to uint256) or an ERC-7913 key (hashed with `keccak256(verifier || key)`
and cast to uint256). The caller must encrypt the signerId off-chain before calling this function.

Requirements:

* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---) for additional requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-removeSigners-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">removeSigners(euint256[] oldSignersIds)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-removeSigners-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Removes encrypted signerIds from the authorized set for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself. See [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---).

Increments the account's configuration nonce, invalidating any outstanding disclosure entries
made under the previous nonce.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-setThreshold-externalEuint32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setThreshold(externalEuint32 newThreshold, bytes inputProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-setThreshold-externalEuint32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets the encrypted threshold for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself. See [`ERC7579Multisig._setThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-_setThreshold-address-uint64-).

Increments the account's configuration nonce, invalidating any outstanding disclosure entries
made under the previous nonce.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">requestDiscloseOperation(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes[] signingSigners, euint256[] signerIdHandles, bytes[] signatures)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Requests disclosure of the multisignature validation result for an operation (hash).

This function validates the multisignature using encrypted values and makes the
result publicly decryptable. Applications that require using the validation result
must call this function off-chain first to enable the decryption proof to be included
in a subsequent call to `discloseOperation` to verify the proof.

<Callout>
This function does not perform any access control. Anyone can request disclosure
of the multisignature validation result for any operation.
</Callout>

<Callout type="warn">
Each call produces a secret-dependent encrypted boolean that is made publicly
decryptable. A caller who possesses valid signatures for the operation `hash` can
probe arbitrary signer subsets across multiple calls to infer the threshold or weight
distribution, since the decrypted result reveals whether the chosen subset meets the
threshold.
</Callout>

Emits a [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigOperationDiscloseRequested`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigOperationDiscloseRequested-address-address-bytes32-ebool-) event.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">discloseOperation(address relayer, address account, bytes32 hash, bytes decryptionProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Verifies the decryption proof for a disclosed operation (hash).

Requirements:

* The decryption proof must be cryptographically valid. Reverts if invalid.

Emits a [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigOperationDisclosed`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigOperationDisclosed-address-bytes32-ebool-) event.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">discloseOperation(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes decryptionProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Same as [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.discloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes-) but with the relayer set to `msg.sender`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-dismissDisclosure--"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">dismissDisclosure()</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-dismissDisclosure--">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Consumes the caller's configuration nonce, invalidating all outstanding disclosure
entries. Useful when an ERC-1271 signer's validity changes and the account needs to
revoke previously snapshotted authorization results.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, euint256[] newSignerIds)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds the encrypted `newSignerIds` to those allowed to sign on behalf of the `account`.

Requirements:

* The number of signers must not exceed `type(uint32).max`. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners--) if exceeded.

<Callout type="warn">
This function does not increment the account's configuration nonce.
Callers that change the configuration must call `Nonces-_useNonce` to invalidate
outstanding disclosure entries.
</Callout>

<Callout>
FHE encryption is non-deterministic. Encrypting the same plaintext uint256 value multiple times
produces different ciphertexts and thus different handles. This allows the same underlying
signer to be added multiple times, so duplicate detection by comparing encrypted handles
is not possible.
</Callout>

Emits [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigSignerAdded`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigSignerAdded-address-euint256-) for each new signer.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, euint256[] oldSignerIds)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Removes the encrypted `oldSignerIds` from the authorized signerIds for the account.

Requirements:

* Each of `oldSignerIds` must be authorized. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner-bytes-) if not.
* After removal, at least one signer must remain. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners--) if not.

<Callout type="warn">
This function does not increment the account's configuration nonce.
Callers that change the configuration must call `Nonces-_useNonce` to invalidate
outstanding disclosure entries.
</Callout>

<Callout>
The threshold will be automatically capped at the number of remaining signers to ensure it's
always reachable.
</Callout>

Emits [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigSignerRemoved`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigSignerRemoved-address-euint256-) for each removed signer.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_setThreshold-address-euint32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setThreshold(address account, euint32 newThreshold)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_setThreshold-address-euint32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets the encrypted signatures `threshold` required to approve a multisignature operation.

<Callout type="warn">
This function does not increment the account's configuration nonce.
Callers that change the configuration must call `Nonces-_useNonce` to invalidate
outstanding disclosure entries.
</Callout>

<Callout>
The threshold is automatically capped at the current number of signers to ensure it's
always reachable.
</Callout>

Emits [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigThresholdSet`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigThresholdSet-address-euint32-) with the final threshold value.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_maxThreshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_maxThreshold(address account) → euint32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_maxThreshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns the maximum threshold for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_requestDiscloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-ebool-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_requestDiscloseOperation(address relayer, address account, bytes32 hash, ebool valid)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_requestDiscloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-ebool-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Requests disclosure of the multisignature validation result for an operation (hash).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_discloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_discloseOperation(address relayer, address account, bytes32 hash, bytes decryptionProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_discloseOperation-address-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Verifies the decryption proof for a disclosed operation (hash).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_validateMultisig-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateMultisig(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes[] signingSigners, euint256[] signerIdHandles, bytes[] signatures) → ebool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_validateMultisig-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates the multisignature operation by checking the threshold and the signatures.

The validation is performed using encrypted values to maintain confidentiality. The function
verifies that the number of valid signatures meets or exceeds the encrypted threshold.

Where `signingSigners` are the plaintext signers (addresses or ERC-7913 keys as bytes),
`signerIdHandles` are their corresponding encrypted id handles, and `signatures` are their
signatures of the operation `hash`.

<Callout>
Given that both the threshold and signers are encrypted, it's possible to pass-in
signatures that don't count towards the threshold.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateSignatures(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes[] signingSigners, euint256[] signerIdHandles, bytes[] signatures) → euint32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_validateSignatures-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates the signatures using the signers and their corresponding signatures.
Returns the number of valid signatures as an encrypted value.

To count towards the threshold, for each signer:

1. The handle must exist in the authorized set
2. The handle must match the corresponding plaintext signer
   (address cast to uint256 or keccak256 hash for ERC-7913 keys)
3. The signature must be valid for the given hash
4. The signer must not be a duplicate of a previous signer in the array

The signers should be ordered by their uint256 representation to ensure efficient duplication check.
Unordered signers are supported, but the uniqueness check will be more expensive. See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._toId`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_toId-bytes-).

Requirements:

* The `signingSigners`, `signerIdHandles`, and `signatures` arrays must have the same length.
  Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigMismatchedValidationLength`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigMismatchedValidationLength-uint256-uint256-uint256-) if not.

</div>
</div>

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_signerWeight-address-euint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeMultisigInitData(bytes initData) → bytes inputProof, externalEuint256[] signerIds, externalEuint32 _threshold</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_decodeMultisigInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes multisig initialization data from calldata without memory allocation.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes inputProof, externalEuint256[] signerIds, externalEuint32 threshold)`

Requirements:

* The `initData` must be at least 160 bytes long to contain the minimum structure.
* All offsets must be properly aligned and within bounds.
* All array length fields must be accessible within the provided data.
* The proof data must be fully contained within the provided data.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579MultisigWeighted` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579MultisigWeighted.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579MultisigWeighted.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579Multisig`](#ERC7579Multisig) that supports weighted signatures.

This module extends the multisignature module to allow assigning different weights
to each signer, enabling more flexible governance schemes. For example, some guardians
could have higher weight than others, allowing for weighted voting or prioritized authorization.

Example use case:

A smart account with this module installed can schedule social recovery operations
after obtaining approval from guardians with sufficient total weight (e.g., requiring
a total weight of 10, with 3 guardians weighted as 5, 3, and 2), and then execute them
after the time delay has passed.

<Callout type="warn">
When setting a threshold value, ensure it matches the scale used for signer weights.
For example, if signers have weights like 1, 2, or 3, then a threshold of 4 would require
signatures with a total weight of at least 4 (e.g., one with weight 1 and one with weight 3).
</Callout>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
Besides the standard delay and signer configuration, this can also include
signer weights.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold, uint64[] weights)`

<Callout>
An account can only call onInstall once. If called directly by the account,
the signer will be set to the provided data. Future installations will revert with
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

<Callout>
This function skips `super.onInstall` to avoid setting the threshold during installation
since it can be greater than the total amount of signers. This function adds the signers, sets the weights,
and sets the threshold.
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData(bytes initData) → bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold_, uint64[] weights</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes weighted multisig initialization data from calldata without memory allocation.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold, uint64[] weights)`

Requirements:

* The `initData` must be at least 160 bytes long to contain the minimum ABI-encoded structure.
* The signers array offset must be properly aligned and within bounds.
* The weights array offset must be properly aligned and within bounds.
* The array length fields must be accessible within the provided data.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_setSignerWeights-address-bytes---uint64---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setSignerWeights(address account, bytes[] signers, uint64[] weights)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_setSignerWeights-address-bytes---uint64---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets weights for multiple signers at once. Internal version without access control.

Requirements:

* `signers` and `weights` arrays must have the same length. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigMismatchedLength`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigMismatchedLength--) on mismatch.
* Each signer must exist in the set of authorized signers. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner-bytes-) if not.
* Each weight must be greater than 0. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigInvalidWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigInvalidWeight-bytes-uint64-) if not.
* See [`ERC7579Multisig._validateReachableThreshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-_validateReachableThreshold-address-) for the threshold validation.

Emits [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-) for each signer.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_addSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, bytes[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_addSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to add weight tracking. See [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---).
Each new signer has a default weight of 1.

In cases where [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) is almost `type(uint64).max` (due to a large `_totalExtraWeight`), adding new
signers could cause the [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) computation to overflow. Adding a [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) call after the new
signers are added ensures no such overflow happens.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_removeSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, bytes[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_removeSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to handle weight tracking during removal. See [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---).

Just like [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---), this function does not emit [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-) events. The
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7913SignerRemoved`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7913SignerRemoved-address-bytes-) event emitted by [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---) is enough to track weights here.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_validateReachableThreshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateReachableThreshold(address account)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_validateReachableThreshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to validate threshold against total weight instead of signer count.

<Callout>
This function intentionally does not call `super._validateReachableThreshold` because the base implementation
assumes each signer has a weight of 1, which is a subset of this weighted implementation. Consider that multiple
implementations of this function may exist in the contract, so important side effects may be missed
depending on the linearization order.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_validateThreshold-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateThreshold(address account, bytes[] validatingSigners) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_validateThreshold-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates that the total weight of signers meets the [`ERC7579Multisig.threshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-) requirement.
Overrides the base implementation to use weights instead of count.

<Callout>
This function intentionally does not call `super._validateThreshold` because the base implementation
assumes each signer has a weight of 1, which is incompatible with this weighted implementation.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged(address indexed account, bytes indexed signer, uint64 weight)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">event</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-">#</a>
</div>
</div>

<div className="px-4">

Emitted when a signer's weight is changed.

<Callout>
Not emitted in [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---) or [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---). Indexers must rely on [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7913SignerAdded`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7913SignerAdded-address-bytes-)
and [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7913SignerRemoved`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7913SignerRemoved-address-bytes-) to index a default weight of 1. See [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.signerWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-signerWeight-address-bytes-).
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Thrown when a signer's weight is invalid.

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential) that supports weighted signatures.

This module extends the confidential multisignature module to allow assigning different weights
to each signer, enabling more flexible governance schemes. For example, some guardians
could have higher weight than others, allowing for weighted voting or prioritized authorization.

Unlike the standard [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted) module, this confidential version uses Fully
Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) to keep signer identities (as uint256), weights, and the threshold
value encrypted on-chain. This provides privacy benefits by hiding the identity of authorized
signers (whether addresses or ERC-7913 keys), their individual weights, and the number of
signatures required to approve operations.

A smart account with this module installed can schedule social recovery operations
after obtaining approval from guardians with sufficient total weight (e.g., requiring
a total weight of 10, with 3 guardians weighted as 5, 3, and 2), while keeping both the
signer identities, weights, and threshold confidential.

<Callout type="warn">
When setting a threshold value, ensure it matches the scale used for signer weights.
For example, if signers have weights like 1, 2, or 3, then a threshold of 4 would require
signatures with a total weight of at least 4 (e.g., one with weight 1 and one with weight 3).
</Callout>

<Callout>
The threshold can be set to 0 given it can't be validated in runtime. The account
operators must rely on external validation to ensure the threshold is not 0, which allows
arbitrary execution.
</Callout>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
Besides the standard signer and threshold configuration, this can also include
encrypted signer weights.

See [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the `initData` encoding details.

<Callout>
An account can only call `onInstall` once. If called directly by the account,
the signers, weights, and threshold will be set to the provided data. Future
installations will revert with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* The `signers` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig.addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---) and [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.setSignerWeights`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-setSignerWeights-bytes---uint64---) for signer and weight configuration requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-setSignerWeights-euint256---externalEuint16---bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setSignerWeights(euint256[] signers, externalEuint16[] externalWeights, bytes inputProof)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-setSignerWeights-euint256---externalEuint16---bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets encrypted weights for signers for the calling account.
Can only be called by the account itself.

Increments the account's configuration nonce, invalidating any outstanding disclosure entries
made under the previous nonce.

Requirements:

* Those of [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._setSignerWeights`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_setSignerWeights-address-bytes---uint64---) for additional requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData(bytes initData) → bytes inputProof, externalEuint256[] signers, externalEuint32 threshold_, externalEuint16[] weights</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes weighted multisig initialization data from calldata without memory allocation.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes inputProof, externalEuint256[] signers, externalEuint32 threshold, externalEuint16[] weights)`

Requirements:

* The `initData` must be at least 224 bytes long to contain the minimum structure.
* All offsets must be properly aligned and within bounds.
* All array length fields must be accessible within the provided data.
* The proof data must be fully contained within the provided data.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, euint256[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to add weight tracking and restrict signer count. See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._addSigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---).
Each new signer has a default weight of 1.

Requirements:

* The number of signers must not exceed `type(uint16).max`. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners--) if exceeded.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_setSignerWeights-address-euint256---euint16---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setSignerWeights(address account, euint256[] signers, euint16[] weights)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_setSignerWeights-address-euint256---euint16---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets encrypted weights for multiple signers at once. Internal version without access control.

Requirements:

* `signers` and `weights` arrays must have the same length. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential.ERC7579MultisigMismatchedSignerWeights`](#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-ERC7579MultisigMismatchedSignerWeights--) on mismatch.
* Each signer must exist in the set of authorized signers. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigNonexistentSigner-bytes-) if not.

<Callout type="warn">
This function does not increment the account's configuration nonce.
Callers that change the configuration must call `Nonces-_useNonce` to invalidate
outstanding disclosure entries.
</Callout>

<Callout>
The threshold will be automatically capped at the total weight to ensure it's always reachable.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Weights of 0 are automatically converted to weight 1 (base weight). See [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.signerWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-signerWeight-address-bytes-).
</Callout>

Emits [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-) for each signer.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, euint256[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to handle weight tracking during removal. See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._removeSigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---).

Cleans up weights for removed signers and updates the total extra weight accordingly.

</div>
</div>

<div className="px-4">

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidential-_signerWeight-address-euint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<a id="ERC7579P256"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579P256` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579P256.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579P256.sol";
```

Implementation of an `IERC7579Module` that uses an P256 signer for signature
validation.

The P256 signer consists of a P256 public key pair (qx, qy coordinates) that
is registered during module installation. Signatures must be valid P256 signatures
that can be verified against this public key.

This module provides a base implementation for P256 signer validation that can be
attached to any function through the [`ERC7579P256._validateP256`](#ERC7579P256-_validateP256-address-bytes32-bytes-) internal function.

<a id="ERC7579P256-signer-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">signer(address account) → bytes32 qx, bytes32 qy</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579P256-signer-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns the authorized P256 signer for the specified account.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579P256-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579P256-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
The `data` parameter should contain the P256 public key coordinates (64 bytes minimum).

If the account doesn't already have a signer configured, the first 64 bytes
of `data` will be used as the authorized P256 signer (qx, qy).
See [`ERC7579ECDSA._extractSigner`](#ERC7579ECDSA-_extractSigner-bytes-) for more details about the encoding.

Requirements:

* The `data` must be at least 64 bytes. Reverts with [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256InvalidSignerLength`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256InvalidSignerLength--) if not.
* The `account` must have no signer installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256AlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256AlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* Decode `data` into `abi.encode(bytes32 qx, bytes32 qy)`.
* The `data` must contain valid P256 public key coordinates. Reverts with [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256InvalidPublicKey`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256InvalidPublicKey-bytes32-bytes32-) if not.

Emits a [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256SignerSet`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256SignerSet-address-bytes32-bytes32-) event if the signer is set.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579P256-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579P256-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up the module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Removes the authorized P256 signer for the calling account.

If the account has a configured signer, it will be cleared. If no signer
is configured, this function will behave as a no-op.

Emits a [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256SignerSet`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256SignerSet-address-bytes32-bytes32-) event if the signer is cleared.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579P256-setSigner-address-bytes32-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setSigner(address account, bytes32 qx, bytes32 qy)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579P256-setSigner-address-bytes32-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets the P256 signer for the specified account.

Requirements:

* The `qx` and `qy` coordinates must not be zero.
* The `qx` and `qy` coordinates must be valid P256 public key coordinates.
* The caller must be the specified account.

Emits a [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256SignerSet`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256SignerSet-address-bytes32-bytes32-) event.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579P256-_setSigner-address-bytes32-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ECDSA.setSigner`](#ERC7579ECDSA-setSigner-address-address-) without access control.

Requirements:

* The `qx` and `qy` coordinates must not be zero.
* The `qx` and `qy` coordinates must be valid P256 public key coordinates.

Emits a [`ERC7579P256.ERC7579P256SignerSet`](#ERC7579P256-ERC7579P256SignerSet-address-bytes32-bytes32-) event.

</div>
</div>
