<a id="ERC7579P256-_validateP256-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateP256(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579P256-_validateP256-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates a P256 signature against the configured signer for the account.

This function extracts the r and s components from the signature and verifies
them against the P256 public key stored for the given account.

The signature is expected to be at least 64 bytes containing the P256 signature
components (r, s) as 32-byte values.

</div>
</div>

## `ERC7579SessionKeys` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579SessionKeys.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579SessionKeys.sol";
```

Abstract base for ERC-7579 session keys.

This module allows registering ERC-7913 keys as session keys on a smart account, each with
a configurable validity range. A session key is any ERC-7913 signer (an arbitrary
cryptographic key identified by a `(verifier, key)` pair) that is registered for a
specific account. The key is only considered active between its `from` and `until`
timepoints.

The key is encoded in the `signature` field of the user operations and ERC-1271 requests for
identification and cryptographic verification. See [`ERC7579SessionKeys._tryDecodeSignature`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-) for the encoding details.

This module provides session key management through the [`ERC7579SessionKeys._validateSessionKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-uint256-bytes32-bytes-) and
[`ERC7579SessionKeys.isValid`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-isValid-address-bytes-) internal functions. Concrete implementations should attach it to validation flows such as
[`ERC7579Validator._rawERC7579Validation`](#ERC7579Validator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-).

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-keys-address-uint256-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">keys(address account, uint256 startIdx, uint256 endIdx) → bytes[]</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-keys-address-uint256-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns a slice of the registered session keys for an `account`.

Using `startIdx = 0` and `endIdx = type(uint256).max` will return all keys.

<Callout type="warn">
Depending on the `startIdx` and `endIdx`, this operation can copy a large amount
of data to memory, which can be expensive. This is designed for view accessors queried
without gas fees. Using it in state-changing functions may become uncallable if the set
grows too large.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
See `IERC7579Module-onInstall`. The `initData` encodes arrays of keys and their
corresponding validity ranges.

See [`ERC7579SessionKeys._decodeKeysInitData`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_decodeKeysInitData-bytes-) for the encoding details.

Requirements:

* Those from [`ERC7579SessionKeys._decodeKeysInitData`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_decodeKeysInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* Those from [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) for the key configuration requirements.

<Callout>
Set `starts[i]` to `0` to activate the key immediately at the current timepoint.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Set `ends[i]` to `0` to register a key with no expiry.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up the module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Increments the account's nonce to invalidate all registered session keys.

See `IERC7579Module-onUninstall`.

<Callout>
This approach avoids unbounded gas costs by not iterating over keys.
Previous key data remains in storage but becomes inaccessible due to the nonce increment.
Upon reinstallation, the module will use a new nonce and ignore old keys.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addKeys(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] keys_, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal function to register session keys for an `account`.

For each key, stores the ERC-7913 key bytes in the enumerable set and records its
validity range. Keys are identified by `keccak256(key)` in the validity mapping.

Requirements:

* All arrays must have equal length. Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysMismatchedInitDataLengths`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysMismatchedInitDataLengths--) if not.
* Each of `keys_` must not be registered. Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysAlreadyRegisteredKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysAlreadyRegisteredKey-bytes-) if it is.

<Callout>
Setting `starts[i]` to `0` resolves to the current timepoint.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Setting `ends[i]` to `0` stores `type(uint48).max`, meaning no expiry.
</Callout>

Emits an [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet-address-bytes-uint48-uint48-) event for each key.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_removeKeys-address-uint256-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeKeys(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] keys_)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_removeKeys-address-uint256-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal function to remove session keys from an `account`.

Removes the key from the enumerable set and clears its validity range in storage.

Requirements:

* Each of `keys_` must be registered. Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey-bytes-) if not.

Emits an [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeyUnset`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeyUnset-address-bytes-) event for each key.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateSessionKey(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates an ERC-7913 session key signature against the registered keys for the account.

Decodes the ERC-7913 key and its cryptographic signature from the encoded `signature`
field, then checks that the key is registered and that the cryptographic signature is
valid. Does not validate the [`ERC7579SessionKeys.validity`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-validity-address-bytes-) range.

See [`ERC7579SessionKeys._tryDecodeSignature`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-) for the encoding details.

<Callout>
Does not validate the [`ERC7579SessionKeys.validity`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-validity-address-bytes-) range.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-uint256-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateSessionKey(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-uint256-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Variant of [`ERC7579SessionKeys._validateSessionKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_validateSessionKey-address-uint256-bytes32-bytes-) that receives an `installation_` nonce.

</div>
</div>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_keyId(bytes key) → bytes32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

</div>
</div>

</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_tryDecodeSignature(bytes signature) → bool success, bytes key, bytes keySignature</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes a session key signature into its ERC-7913 `key` and cryptographic
`keySignature` components.

The `signature` must be ABI-encoded as `abi.encode(bytes key, bytes keySignature)`.

Returns `(false, emptyBytes, emptyBytes)` without reverting if the encoding is invalid
or if any offset or length would read out of bounds.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeys-_decodeKeysInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeKeysInitData(bytes initData) → bytes[] keys_, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeys-_decodeKeysInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes the initialization data for the session keys module.

The `initData` must be ABI-encoded as `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)`
where each array has the same length.

Requirements:

* `initData` must be at least 192 bytes (6 × 32 bytes for the 3 ABI offsets and 3 array lengths).
* All offsets must point within the `initData` bounds.

Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysInvalidInitData`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysInvalidInitData--) if the encoding is invalid.

</div>
</div>

## `ERC7579WebAuthn` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/ERC7579WebAuthn.sol">

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/ERC7579WebAuthn.sol";
```

Implementation of an `IERC7579Module` that uses a WebAuthn credential for signature
validation.

This module provides a base implementation for WebAuthn signature validation that can be
attached to any function through the [`ERC7579WebAuthn._validateWebAuthn`](#ERC7579WebAuthn-_validateWebAuthn-address-bytes32-bytes-) internal function.

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579DelayedExecutor` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/executors/ERC7579DelayedExecutor.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/executors/ERC7579DelayedExecutor.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579Executor`](#ERC7579Executor) that provides time-delayed execution patterns
for smart accounts with scheduling and expiration capabilities.

This contract provides the basic scheduling functionality through the [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._schedule`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_schedule-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-),
[`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._scheduleAt`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_scheduleAt-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-uint48-uint32-), and [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._cancel`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_cancel-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) functions. Developers must implement their own public
`schedule`, `cancel`, and `execute` functions in derived contracts to define the
specific validation and access control logic for each operation.

#### Operation Lifecycle

1. Scheduling: Operations are scheduled via [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._schedule`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_schedule-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) with a specified delay period.
The delay period is set during [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-) and can be customized via [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.setDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-). Each
operation enters a `Scheduled` state and must wait for its delay period to elapse.

2. Security Window: During the delay period, operations remain in `Scheduled` state but
cannot be executed. Through this period, suspicious operations can be monitored and
canceled via [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._cancel`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_cancel-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) if appropriate.

3. Execution & Expiration: Once the delay period elapses, operations transition to `Ready` state.
Operations can be executed via [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._execute`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_execute-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) and have an expiration period after becoming
executable. If an operation is not executed within the expiration period, it becomes `Expired`
and can't be executed. Expired operations must be rescheduled with a different salt.

#### Delay Management

Accounts can set their own delay periods during installation or via [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.setDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-).
The delay period is enforced even between installs and uninstalls to prevent
immediate downgrades. When setting a new delay period, the transition period before
the new delay takes effect depends on the direction of the change:

* When _increasing_ the delay, the new value takes effect after `min(newDelay, delayChangeWindow())`.
* When _decreasing_ the delay, the new value takes effect after `currentDelay - newDelay`.

<Callout>
Use [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._scheduleAt`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_scheduleAt-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-uint48-uint32-) to schedule operations at specific points in time. This is
useful to pre-schedule operations for non-deployed accounts (e.g. subscriptions).
</Callout>

<Callout>
Scheduling, executing and cancelling doesn't have any access control by default
and should be validated accordingly while extending this contract.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Target addresses of an operation may return an arbitrary large amount of data, which
may cause the operation to fail due to insufficient gas. Consider that a target could return
more data than initially expected (e.g. after being upgraded, or when their return data
depends on state), potentially blocking the operation's execution until they expire.
</Callout>

Maximum transition period applied when [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.setDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-) increases the delay, which takes effect
after `min(newDelay, delayChangeWindow())`. Delay decreases are unaffected and take effect after
`currentDelay - newDelay`. Also used as the default delay if not provided during [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-).

<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
The account calling this function becomes registered with the module.

The `initData` must be `abi.encodePacked(uint32(initialDelay), uint32(initialExpiration))`.
Otherwise, the delay will be set to [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.delayChangeWindow`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-delayChangeWindow--) and [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.defaultExpiration`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-defaultExpiration--) respectively.

If a previous delay is still active (e.g. from a prior installation that was uninstalled before its
delay reset completed), the same asymmetric transition rules as [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.setDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-) apply to the new delay,
preventing an uninstall/reinstall cycle from being used to bypass the security window.

Requirements:

* The module must not be already installed on the account. Reverts with [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.ERC7579DelayedExecutorAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-ERC7579DelayedExecutorAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* `initData` must be empty or decode correctly to `(uint32, uint32)`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setDelay(uint32 newDelay)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Allows an account to update its execution delay (see [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.getDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-getDelay-address-)).

The transition period before the new delay takes effect depends on the direction of the change:

* When _increasing_ the delay, the new value takes effect after `min(newDelay, delayChangeWindow())`.
  For example, increasing from 1 day to 3 days takes effect after 3 days. Increasing from
  1 day to 10 days takes effect after [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.delayChangeWindow`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-delayChangeWindow--) (5 days). This cap prevents the account
  from being locked out by an accidental large increase (e.g. using milliseconds instead of
  seconds).
* When _decreasing_ the delay, the new value takes effect after `currentDelay - newDelay`.
  This guarantees that a recovery operation cannot execute faster than the delay that was
  active at the moment the change was scheduled. For example, decreasing from 10 days to
  3 days takes effect after 7 days.

Can only be called by the account itself.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setExpiration-uint32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">setExpiration(uint32 newExpiration)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setExpiration-uint32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Allows an account to update its execution expiration (see [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.getExpiration`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-getExpiration-address-)).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up the [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.getDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-getDelay-address-) and [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.getExpiration`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-getExpiration-address-) values by scheduling them to `0`
and respecting the previous delay and expiration values.

<Callout type="warn">
This function does not clean up scheduled operations. This means operations
could potentially be re-executed if the module is reinstalled later. This is a deliberate
design choice for efficiency, but module implementations may want to override this behavior
to clear scheduled operations during uninstallation for their specific use cases.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Uninstalling the module schedules the delay to reset to `0` after the current delay
(equivalent to calling `setDelay(0)`), and immediately removes the expiration. If the module
is reinstalled before the delay reset completes, the same asymmetric transition rules as
[`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.setDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-setDelay-uint32-) apply to prevent bypassing the security window through an uninstall/reinstall cycle.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_schedule-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_schedule(address account, bytes32 salt, bytes32 mode, bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_schedule-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Schedules an operation to be executed after the account's delay period (see [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.getDelay`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-getDelay-address-)).
Operations are uniquely identified by the combination of `account`, `salt`, `mode`, and `data`.
This should be called by derived contracts after performing their custom validation logic.

Requirements:

* The module must be installed on the account. Reverts with [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.ERC7579ExecutorModuleNotInstalled`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-ERC7579ExecutorModuleNotInstalled--) if not.

<Callout>
This function does not have any access control validation.
</Callout>

</div>

Check that the current state of an operation matches the requirements described by the `allowedStates` bitmap.
This bitmap should be built using [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._encodeStateBitmap`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_encodeStateBitmap-enum-ERC7579DelayedExecutor-OperationState-).

If requirements are not met, reverts with a [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.ERC7579ExecutorUnexpectedOperationState`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-ERC7579ExecutorUnexpectedOperationState-bytes32-enum-ERC7579DelayedExecutor-OperationState-bytes32-) error.

<Callout>
Passing a `bytes32(0)` for `allowedStates` will always revert.
</Callout>

<a id="ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_encodeStateBitmap-enum-ERC7579DelayedExecutor-OperationState-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_encodeStateBitmap(enum ERC7579DelayedExecutor.OperationState operationState) → bytes32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_encodeStateBitmap-enum-ERC7579DelayedExecutor-OperationState-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Encodes a `OperationState` into a `bytes32` representation where each bit enabled corresponds to
the underlying position in the `OperationState` enum. For example:

```
0x00...100000
  ^^^^^^------ ...
       ^------ Canceled
        ^----- Executed
         ^---- Expired
          ^--- Ready
           ^-- Scheduled
            ^- Unknown
```

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579ERC20Spender` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/executors/ERC7579ERC20Spender.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/executors/ERC7579ERC20Spender.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579SessionKeysValidator`](#ERC7579SessionKeysValidator) that provides ERC20 token spending limits
granted to session keys with automatic renewal periods for ERC-7579 smart accounts.

This module allows accounts to configure spending allowances for external spenders,
enabling use cases like subscription services, recurring payments, or delegated spending
with predefined limits.

Same dual-role design as [`ERC7579NativeSpender`](#ERC7579NativeSpender): a session key authorizes a UserOp whose
`callData` targets [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) on this module, and the module
spends through `executeFromExecutor` on the account.

Each `(session key, ERC-20 token)` pair carries its own `(limit, renewPeriod)` spending
policy. A single key can have policies for multiple tokens by listing it multiple times
(with different tokens) in the executor install initData.

<Callout>
The module should be installed under both module types, in this order:

1. **Validator install (first)**: `initData` is `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)`.
   Registers session keys with their validity ranges through [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---).
   At least one key must be registered, otherwise the install reverts.
2. **Executor install (second)**: `initData` is
   `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, address[] tokens, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)`.
   Writes a spending policy for each `(keys[i], tokens[i])` row. Each row's `keys[i]` MUST
   already be registered. Keys MAY repeat across rows to configure multiple tokens for the
   same key. The `keys` array MUST be non-empty.

Once any `(key, token)` config is written, the module is "installed" and further `onInstall`
calls revert with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderAlreadyInstalled--). To change the spending policy, the
account must uninstall and reinstall (which bumps the installation nonce and resets state).
</Callout>

<Callout type="warn">
This module deliberately disables ERC-1271 signing
([`ERC7579ERC20Spender.isValidSignatureWithSender`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-) always returns `0xffffffff`) so a spending session key cannot be
repurposed to authorize arbitrary off-chain messages on behalf of the account.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Removing a session key via [`ERC7579SessionKeys.removeKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-removeKeys-bytes---) clears its validity range but
does NOT clear the `(key, token)` spending configs. If the same key bytes are re-registered via
[`ERC7579SessionKeys.addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-addKeys-bytes---uint48---uint48---) (without `addKeysWithConfig`), the previously-stored configs become
active again. To re-register a key with a fresh policy, call [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.addKeysWithConfig`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-addKeysWithConfig-bytes---uint48---uint48---address---uint160---uint48---) instead.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Set `starts[i] == 0` to schedule a key from the current timepoint.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Set `renewPeriod == type(uint48).max` to effectively disable the renewal period.
</Callout>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">spent(address account, bytes key, address token, uint256 period) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

</div>
</div>

</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-spent-address-bytes-address-uint256-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">spent(address account, bytes key, address token, uint256 period, uint256 start) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-spent-address-bytes-address-uint256-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-spendingAt-address-bytes-address-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">spendingAt(address account, bytes key, address token, uint256 period) → uint256 spending, uint256 available</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-spendingAt-address-bytes-address-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns `(spending, available)` for `(key, token)` at `period`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-periodAt-address-bytes-address-uint48-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">periodAt(address account, bytes key, address token, uint48 timepoint) → uint256 start, uint256 period</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-periodAt-address-bytes-address-uint48-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns `(start, period)` for `(key, token)` at `timepoint`.

Requirements:

* `timepoint` must be `>=` the key's `from`. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotStarted`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotStarted-address-bytes32-address-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The spending config must be set. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotSet`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotSet-address-bytes32-address-) otherwise.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.

This module is installed under BOTH module types and `onInstall` is called twice. The
shape of `initData` differs between the two calls:

* **First call** (validator install, `installed(msg.sender) == false`):
  `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)`.
* **Second call** (executor install, `installed(msg.sender) == true`):
  `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, address[] tokens, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)`.

First call requirements:

* The `keys` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderEmptyKeys`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderEmptyKeys--) otherwise.
* Those from the parent's [`ERC7579SessionKeys.onInstall`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-onInstall-bytes-) (key registration).

Second call requirements:

* No `(key, token)` config has been written yet under this installation. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderAlreadyInstalled--) otherwise.
* `initData.length` must be at least 256 bytes (4 ABI offsets + 4 array lengths). Reverts
  with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidInitData`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidInitData--) otherwise. All offsets must point within bounds.
* All four arrays must have equal length.
* The `keys` array must be non-empty.
* Each `keys[i]` must already be registered. Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey-bytes-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._setConfig`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-) for the spending config requirements.

Emits [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet-address-bytes-uint48-uint48-) on the first call and [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigSet`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigSet-address-bytes-address-uint256-uint256-)
for each row on the second call.

<Callout>
Each start will be set to the current timepoint if it's 0.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-addKeysWithConfig-bytes---uint48---uint48---address---uint160---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">addKeysWithConfig(bytes[] newKeys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends, address[] tokens_, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-addKeysWithConfig-bytes---uint48---uint48---address---uint160---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds new session keys with one spending configuration each, in a single call. Can only
be called by the account itself.

Requirements:

* All six arrays must have equal length.
* Those from [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) (and the [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) override's past-start ban).
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._setConfig`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-) for each row.

<Callout>
This helper only supports one `(token, limit, renewPeriod)` per new key. To configure
multiple tokens for a single key, list it multiple times in the executor install initData.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">transferFrom(address account, bytes key, address token, address to, uint256 amount)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Transfers `token` from `account` to `to` on behalf of `key`.

Tracks the spending limit for the `(key, token)` pair on `account`, updates the spent
amount for the current period, and executes the transfer through the account's
`IERC7579Execution-executeFromExecutor`.

Two authorization paths are accepted:

1. **Executor flow**: `msg.sender == account`. The validator has already verified the session
   key's signature and the calldata shape, so no further checks are performed here.
2. **EOA self-call**: `key.length == 20` and `address(bytes20(key)) == msg.sender`. The key's
   validity range is enforced manually.

Requirements:

* The caller is authorized via one of the two paths above. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderUnauthorized`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderUnauthorized-address-bytes-) otherwise.
* `amount` must not exceed the available limit for the current period. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20InsufficientAvailable`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20InsufficientAvailable-address-bytes-address-uint256-) otherwise.
* The token's `transfer` must return `true` or have no return value. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderFailedTransfer`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderFailedTransfer-address-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._periodAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_periodAt-address-uint256-bytes32-address-uint48-) for the current timepoint.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">batchTransferFrom(address account, bytes key, address token, address[] tos, uint256[] amounts)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Transfers `token` from `account` to multiple recipients in a single batch.

Same authorization paths as [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-). The available limit check is performed once
against the total of `amounts`.

Requirements:

* `tos.length == amounts.length`. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderMismatchedBatchLengths`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderMismatchedBatchLengths-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The caller is authorized via one of the two paths described in [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-).
* The sum of `amounts` must not exceed the available limit.
* Each ERC20 `transfer` must succeed (return value handled identically to [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-)).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">isValidSignatureWithSender(address, bytes32, bytes) → bytes4</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Disables ERC-1271 signing by session keys. See [`ERC7579NativeSpender.isValidSignatureWithSender`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-)
for rationale.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateUserOp(struct PackedUserOperation userOp, bytes32 userOpHash) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates a user operation. Adds a callData shape gate on top of session-key validation:

`userOp.callData` MUST call `IERC7579Execution.execute(mode, executionCalldata)` where:

* `mode` is `CALLTYPE_SINGLE`.
* `executionCalldata` decodes to `(target=address(this), value=0, data)`.
* `data` calls [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---).
* The `key` parameter inside `data` matches the key in `userOp.signature`.

Returns `ERC4337Utils.SIG_VALIDATION_FAILED` for any shape or key mismatch, otherwise delegates
to the parent for cryptographic verification and validity-range packing.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addKeys(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override of [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) that disallows registering a key with a past
`start` timepoint. See [`ERC7579NativeSpender._addKeys`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) for the full rationale.

Requirements:

* Each `starts[i]` must be either `0` or `>= clock()`. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidStart`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidStart-uint256-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---).

<Callout>
Pass `starts[i] == 0` to resolve the start to the current timepoint.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.periodAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-periodAt-address-bytes-address-uint48-) with explicit `installation_`.

Requirements:

* `timepoint` must be `>=` the key's `from`. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotStarted`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotStarted-address-bytes32-address-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The spending config must be set. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotSet`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigNotSet-address-bytes32-address-) otherwise.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-_checkAccountOrKeySender-address-uint256-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_checkAccountOrKeySender-address-uint256-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Authorizes a [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) / [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) call.

Accepts `msg.sender` as the account itself (executor flow, validator already verified) or as
a 20-byte EOA matching `key` (EOA self-call, validity is checked manually).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setConfig(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes key, address token, uint160 newLimit, uint48 newRenewPeriod)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Writes a spending config for `(keyId, token)` and adds the token to the per-installation
token set.

Requirements:

* `newRenewPeriod > 0`. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidRenewPeriod`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidRenewPeriod-uint256-) otherwise.
* `newLimit > 0`. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidLimit`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidLimit-uint256-) otherwise.
* `token` must be a contract. Reverts with [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidToken`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpenderInvalidToken-address-) otherwise.

Emits [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigSet`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-ERC7579ERC20SpendingConfigSet-address-bytes-address-uint256-uint256-).

</div>
</div>

<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_isAuthorizedCallData-struct-PackedUserOperation-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether `userOp.callData` is a permitted call into [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or
[`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) on this module, and whether its `key` argument matches the one
decoded from `userOp.signature`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Executor"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579Executor` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/executors/ERC7579Executor.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/executors/ERC7579Executor.sol";
```

Abstract executor module for ERC-7579 accounts.

This contract provides the base execution functionality for ERC-7579 accounts.
Developers must implement the [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-) and [`ERC7579ECDSA.onUninstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onUninstall-bytes-) functions, as well as
wrap [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._execute`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_execute-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) in a custom external function in derived contracts to define the
specific validation and access control logic.

Example implementation:

```solidity
contract MyExecutor is ERC7579Executor {
    function execute(
        address account,
        bytes32 salt,
        bytes32 mode,
        bytes calldata data
    ) public virtual returns (bytes[] memory) {
        // Implement your validation logic here
        // e.g., signature verification, access control, time delays, etc.

        // Then call the internal _execute function
        return _execute(account, salt, mode, data);
    }
}
```

<Callout>
For a more advanced implementation with time-delayed execution patterns and
security features, see [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor).
</Callout>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579Executor-_execute-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal function to execute an operation through the account's executor interface.

Emits [`ERC7579Executor.ERC7579ExecutorOperationExecuted`](#ERC7579Executor-ERC7579ExecutorOperationExecuted-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) event.

<Callout>
Target addresses included in the `executionCalldata` may return a large amount of data,
which may cause the operation to fail due to insufficient gas given `executeFromExecutor` copies
all return datas to memory.
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must implement the `IERC7579Execution-executeFromExecutor` function.

<Callout>
This function does not have any access control validation.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

## `ERC7579NativeSpender` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/executors/ERC7579NativeSpender.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/executors/ERC7579NativeSpender.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579SessionKeysValidator`](#ERC7579SessionKeysValidator) that provides native token (ETH) spending
limits granted to session keys with automatic renewal periods for ERC-7579 smart accounts.

This module allows accounts to configure spending allowances for external spenders,
enabling use cases like subscription services, recurring payments, or delegated spending
with predefined limits.

The module is both a validator and an executor:

* As a **validator**, it authorizes user operations signed by a registered session key whose
  `callData` is a single call into this module's [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---). The
  key's validity range is packed into `validationData` so the EntryPoint enforces it natively.
* As an **executor**, it spends from the account through `executeFromExecutor`, decrementing
  the per-key, per-period limit.

Each session key carries its own `(limit, renewPeriod)` spending policy. The start of the
spending periods is the session key's `from` timepoint. Periods automatically renew every
`renewPeriod` time units, allowing the same spending capacity to be used again without
requiring manual intervention. It implements ERC-6372 clock functions.

<Callout>
The module should be installed under both module types, in this order:

1. **Validator install (first)**: `initData` is `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)`.
   This registers session keys with their validity ranges through [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---).
   At least one key must be registered, otherwise the install reverts.
2. **Executor install (second)**: `initData` is `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)`.
   This writes a spending policy for each already-registered key. The `keys` array MUST be
   non-empty and reconfiguration is banned: once a key has been configured, neither it nor any
   other key can be reconfigured under the current installation. To change the spending policy,
   the account must uninstall and reinstall the module (which bumps the installation nonce and
   resets state).
</Callout>

<Callout type="warn">
This module deliberately disables ERC-1271 signing
([`ERC7579ERC20Spender.isValidSignatureWithSender`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-) always returns `0xffffffff`) so a spending session key cannot be
repurposed to authorize arbitrary off-chain messages on behalf of the account.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Set `start` to a future timepoint to schedule a spending limit that activates later.
</Callout>

<Callout>
Set [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.renewPeriod`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-renewPeriod-address-bytes-address-) to `type(uint48).max` to disable the renewal period.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns `(start, period)` for `key` on `account` at `timepoint`.

Requirements:

* `timepoint` must be `>=` the key's `from`. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotStarted`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotStarted-address-bytes32-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The spending config must be set. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotSet`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotSet-address-bytes32-) otherwise.

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.

This module is installed under BOTH module types and `onInstall` is called twice. The
shape of `initData` differs between the two calls:

* **First call** (validator install, `installed(msg.sender) == false`):
  `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)`.
* **Second call** (executor install, `installed(msg.sender) == true`):
  `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)`.

First call requirements:

* The `keys` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderEmptyKeys`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderEmptyKeys--) otherwise.
* Those from the parent's [`ERC7579SessionKeys.onInstall`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-onInstall-bytes-) (key registration).

Second call requirements:

* The account must not have configured the keys yet. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderAlreadyInstalled--) otherwise.
* Those of [`ERC7579NativeSpender._decodeKeyConfigsInitData`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-_decodeKeyConfigsInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* All arrays must have equal length. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedInitDataLengths`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedInitDataLengths-uint256-uint256-uint256-) if not.
* The `keys` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderEmptyKeys`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderEmptyKeys--) otherwise.
* Each key must be registered. Reverts with [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysNonexistentKey-bytes-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._setConfig`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-) for the spending config requirements.

Emits [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet-address-bytes-uint48-uint48-) on the first call and [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet-address-bytes-uint256-uint256-)
for each entry on the second call.

<Callout>
Each start will be set to the current timepoint if it's 0.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-addKeysWithConfig-bytes---uint48---uint48---uint160---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">addKeysWithConfig(bytes[] newKeys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-addKeysWithConfig-bytes---uint48---uint48---uint160---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Adds new session keys with their spending configurations in a single call. Can only be
called by the account itself. See [`ERC7579NativeSpender._addKeysWithConfig`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeysWithConfig-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---uint160---uint48---) for requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">transferFrom(address account, bytes key, address to, uint256 amount)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Transfers native tokens from `account` to `to` on behalf of `key`.

This function tracks the spending limit for the `key` on the `account` and updates
the spent amount for the current period before executing the transfer through the account's
`IERC7579Execution-executeFromExecutor` function.

Two authorization paths are accepted:

1. **Executor flow**: `msg.sender == account`. The validator has already verified the session
   key's signature and the calldata shape, so no further checks are performed here.
2. **EOA self-call**: `key.length == 20` and `address(bytes20(key)) == msg.sender`. The key's
   validity range is enforced manually.

Requirements:

* The caller is authorized via one of the two paths above. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderUnauthorized`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderUnauthorized-address-bytes-) otherwise.
* `amount` must not exceed the available limit for the current period. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeInsufficientAvailable`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeInsufficientAvailable-address-bytes-uint256-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._periodAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_periodAt-address-uint256-bytes32-address-uint48-) for the current timepoint.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address---uint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">batchTransferFrom(address account, bytes key, address[] tos, uint256[] amounts)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address---uint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Transfers native tokens from `account` to multiple recipients in a single batch.

Same authorization paths as [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-). The available limit check is performed once
against the total of `amounts`.

Requirements:

* `tos.length == amounts.length`. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedBatchLengths`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedBatchLengths-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The caller is authorized via one of the two paths described in [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-).
* The sum of `amounts` must not exceed the available limit.
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._periodAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_periodAt-address-uint256-bytes32-address-uint48-) for the current timepoint.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">isValidSignatureWithSender(address, bytes32, bytes) → bytes4</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Disables ERC-1271 signing by session keys.

A spending session key is scoped to authorize [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) / [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) user
operations on this module. Letting it sign arbitrary ERC-1271 digests (e.g. EIP-2612
permits) would let a compromised key bypass the spending bound entirely.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateUserOp(struct PackedUserOperation userOp, bytes32 userOpHash) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates a user operation. Adds a callData shape gate on top of session-key validation:

`userOp.callData` MUST call `IERC7579Execution.execute(mode, executionCalldata)` where:

* `mode` is `CALLTYPE_SINGLE` (other call types are rejected).
* `executionCalldata` decodes to `(target=address(this), value=0, data)`.
* `data` calls [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---).
* The `key` parameter inside `data` matches the key in `userOp.signature` (prevents one key's
  signature from charging another key's allowance).

Returns `ERC4337Utils.SIG_VALIDATION_FAILED` for any shape or key mismatch, otherwise delegates
to the parent for cryptographic verification and validity-range packing.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addKeys(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override of [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) that disallows registering a key with a past
`start` timepoint.

The spending bookkeeping in [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._spentFor`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_spentFor-mapping-address----mapping-uint256----mapping-bytes32----mapping-address----mapping-uint256----mapping-uint256----uint256------) is indexed by `(keyId, start, period)`. Letting a
removed-then-re-registered key pick a past `start` would let it inherit `_spentFor` slots
written under a previous validity range, which (combined with a different `renewPeriod`)
can collide with previously-used period slots. Strictly incremental `start` values keep
each registration's accounting in its own slot space.

Requirements:

* Each `starts[i]` must be either `0` or `>= clock()`. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidStart`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidStart-uint256-) otherwise.
* Those from [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---).

<Callout>
Pass `starts[i] == 0` to resolve the start to the current timepoint.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_removeKeys-address-uint256-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeKeys(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] oldKeys)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_removeKeys-address-uint256-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Removes session keys from the calling account. Also clears the spending configuration
for each key.

<Callout>
Spending history (per-period `spent` values) is preserved in storage at the historical
start timepoint. It is no longer surfaced through `spent-address-bytes-uint256--` (which uses
the current [`ERC7579SessionKeys.validity`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-validity-address-bytes-)'s `from`, now zero) but can still be read via
`spent-address-bytes-uint256-uint256--` with the historical start.
</Callout>

Emits [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeyUnset`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeyUnset-address-bytes-) (via the parent) and [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigUnset`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigUnset-address-bytes-)
for each key.

</div>
</div>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_spent-address-uint256-bytes32-uint256-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_spent(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes32 keyId, uint256 period, uint256 start) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_spent-address-uint256-bytes32-uint256-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.spent`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-spent-address-bytes-address-uint256-uint256-) with explicit `start`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_spendingAt-address-uint256-bytes32-uint256-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_spendingAt(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes32 keyId, uint256 period, uint256 start) → uint256 spending, uint256 available</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_spendingAt-address-uint256-bytes32-uint256-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.spendingAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-spendingAt-address-bytes-address-uint256-) with explicit `start`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_periodAt-address-uint256-bytes32-uint48-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_periodAt(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes32 keyId, uint48 timepoint) → uint256 start, uint256 period</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_periodAt-address-uint256-bytes32-uint48-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.periodAt`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-periodAt-address-bytes-address-uint48-) with explicit `installation_`.

Requirements:

* `timepoint` must be `>=` the key's `from`. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotStarted`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotStarted-address-bytes32-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* The spending config must be set. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotSet`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigNotSet-address-bytes32-) otherwise.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_checkAccountOrKeySender-address-uint256-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_checkAccountOrKeySender(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes key)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_checkAccountOrKeySender-address-uint256-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Authorizes a [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) / [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) call.

Accepts msg.sender as the account itself (executor flow, validator already verified) or as
a 20-byte EOA matching `key` (EOA self-call, validity is checked manually).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_transferFrom-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_transferFrom(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes key, address to, uint256 amount)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_transferFrom-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) without access control.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_batchTransferFrom-address-uint256-bytes-address---uint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_batchTransferFrom(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes key, address[] tos, uint256[] amounts)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_batchTransferFrom-address-uint256-bytes-address---uint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal version of [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) without access control.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-uint160-uint48-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_setConfig(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes key, uint160 newLimit, uint48 newRenewPeriod)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-uint160-uint48-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Internal function to set the spending configuration for a key.

Reconfiguration is banned at the [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-) level, so this function is only ever invoked
with a freshly-registered (or freshly-reinstalled) key whose existing config slot is zero.

Requirements:

* Renewal periods must be greater than zero. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidRenewPeriod`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidRenewPeriod-uint256-) if not.
* Limits must be greater than zero. Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidLimit`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidLimit-uint256-) if not.

Emits a [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet-address-bytes-uint256-uint256-) event.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeysWithConfig-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---uint160---uint48---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addKeysWithConfig(address account, uint256 installation_, bytes[] keys, uint48[] starts, uint48[] ends, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_addKeysWithConfig-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---uint160---uint48---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Registers keys with validity range AND spending config in one pass.

Requirements:

* `newKeys.length == limits.length == renewPeriods.length`. Reverts with
  [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedInitDataLengths`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderMismatchedInitDataLengths-uint256-uint256-uint256-) otherwise.
* Those from the parent's [`ERC7579SessionKeys._addKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_addKeys-address-uint256-bytes---uint48---uint48---) (key registration).
* Those from [`ERC7579ERC20Spender._setConfig`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-_setConfig-address-uint256-bytes-address-uint160-uint48-) for the spending config requirements.

Emits [`ERC7579SessionKeys.ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-ERC7579SessionKeysERC7913KeySet-address-bytes-uint48-uint48-) and [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpendingConfigSet-address-bytes-uint256-uint256-) for each key.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_isAuthorizedCallData-struct-PackedUserOperation-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_isAuthorizedCallData(struct PackedUserOperation userOp) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_isAuthorizedCallData-struct-PackedUserOperation-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether `userOp.callData` is a permitted call into [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or
[`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---) on this module, and whether its `key` argument matches the one
decoded from `userOp.signature`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateSingleExecutionCalldata-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateSingleExecutionCalldata(bytes callData) → bool isSingleAndValid, bytes exec</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateSingleExecutionCalldata-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes the outer `IERC7579Execution.execute(SINGLE, executionCalldata)` call.

Returns `(false, "")` if the call doesn't match the expected shape.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateTransferKey-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateTransferKey(bytes exec) → bool success, bytes callDataKey</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_validateTransferKey-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Verifies the inner single execution targets this module with zero value, calls
[`ERC7579ERC20Spender.transferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-transferFrom-address-bytes-address-address-uint256-) or [`ERC7579ERC20Spender.batchTransferFrom`](#ERC7579ERC20Spender-batchTransferFrom-address-bytes-address-address---uint256---), and returns the `key` argument extracted from the
inner calldata.

Returns `(false, "")` if any check fails.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579NativeSpender-_decodeKeyConfigsInitData-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_decodeKeyConfigsInitData(bytes initData) → bytes[] keys, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579NativeSpender-_decodeKeyConfigsInitData-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Decodes the initialization data for the second install of the module.

The `initData` must be ABI-encoded as `abi.encode(bytes[] keys, uint160[] limits, uint48[] renewPeriods)`
where each array has the same length.

Requirements:

* `initData` must be at least 192 bytes (6 × 32 bytes for the 3 ABI offsets and 3 array lengths).
* All offsets must point within the `initData` bounds.

Reverts with [`ERC7579NativeSpender.ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidInitData`](#ERC7579NativeSpender-ERC7579NativeSpenderInvalidInitData--) if the encoding is invalid.

</div>
</div>
