<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/executors/ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/executors/ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.sol";
```

Social recovery executor module for ERC-7579 accounts.

This module combines weighted multisignatures with time-delayed execution to provide
a secure and flexible social recovery mechanism. Trusted guardians can collectively schedule
recovery operations, which execute after a security delay, giving the legitimate account owner
time to detect and cancel malicious recovery attempts.

To schedule operations, guardians must sign an EIP-712 typed data hash of the operation using
the account's domain separator and provide it to the [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.startRecovery`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-startRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-bytes-) function. Once scheduled,
operations must wait for the configured delay period before becoming executable via [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.executeRecovery`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-executeRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-).

Operations can be canceled either by the account itself or by guardians via [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.cancelRecovery`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-cancelRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-)
with a valid multisignature.

See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor) and [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted) for more details.

<Callout>
This module does not restrict execution by default once the social recovery operation
is ready. Anyone can call [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.executeRecovery`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-executeRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) once the delay period has elapsed.
</Callout>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Installs the social recovery executor module on an account.

This function configures both the delayed executor and weighted multisig components
required for the social recovery mechanism. The installation data must contain
configuration for both components in the specified encoding format.

Data encoding format: `[uint16(executorArgsLength), executorArgs, uint16(multisigArgsLength), multisigArgs]`

Requirements:

* See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.onInstall`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-onInstall-bytes-) for delay/expiration requirements
* See [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.onInstall`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-onInstall-bytes-) for guardian configuration requirements

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Uninstalls the social recovery executor module from an account.

This cleans up both the delayed executor configuration and all guardian-related state.
After uninstallation, all social recovery capabilities are removed.

<Callout type="warn">
Ensure alternative recovery mechanisms or access methods are in place
before uninstalling this module. Uninstalling social recovery without a backup
access method could result in account lockout if primary credentials are lost.
</Callout>

See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.onUninstall`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-) and [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.onUninstall`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-onUninstall-bytes-).

</div>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-startRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">startRecovery(address account, bytes32 salt, bytes32 mode, bytes recoveryData, bytes multisignature)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-startRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Schedules a social recovery operation for the specified account.

The `recoveryData` is the encoded execution that will be sent to the account, and it
follows the ERC-7579 standard encoding. The `multisignature` is a valid authorization
from the guardians to schedule the operation and must be signed over the
[`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.SCHEDULE_RECOVER_TYPEHASH`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-SCHEDULE_RECOVER_TYPEHASH-bytes32) typed data hash of the operation using the account's domain
separator.

Requirements:

* The multisignature must be valid and meet the threshold requirements.
  Reverts with [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutorInvalidSchedule`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutorInvalidSchedule--) if not.
* The signature must be encoded properly. See `ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_validateMultisig`.
* See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._schedule`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_schedule-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) for scheduling requirements.
* The `account` must implement `IERC5267`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-executeRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">executeRecovery(address account, bytes32 salt, bytes32 mode, bytes recoveryData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-executeRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Executes a social recovery operation for the specified account.

This function can be called by any account once the operation is ready (i.e., the delay
period has elapsed). The operation must be in the `Ready` state to be executed.

Requirements:

* See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._execute`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_execute-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) for execution requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-cancelRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">cancelRecovery(address account, bytes32 salt, bytes32 mode, bytes recoveryData, bytes multisignature)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-cancelRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cancels a social recovery operation.

This function allows either the account itself or guardians with a valid multisignature
authorization to cancel a scheduled recovery operation. If the caller is not the account,
the `multisignature` must contain valid guardian signatures over the [`ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor.CANCEL_RECOVER_TYPEHASH`](#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-CANCEL_RECOVER_TYPEHASH-bytes32)
typed data hash using the account's domain separator.

Uninstalling the module resets the threshold to zero while scheduled operations are retained
(see [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor.onUninstall`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-onUninstall-bytes-)). Guardian-authorized cancellation is therefore only
available while a guardian configuration is active. The account keeps its own cancellation path
in either case.

Requirements:

* The caller must be the account or a valid `multisignature` must be provided.
* A `multisignature` only authorizes the cancellation while the [`ERC7579Multisig.threshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-) is nonzero.
* See [`ERC7579DelayedExecutor._cancel`](#ERC7579DelayedExecutor-_cancel-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-) for cancellation requirements.
* The `account` must implement `IERC5267`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-cancelRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">

<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SocialRecoveryExecutor-cancelRecovery-address-bytes32-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579ECDSAValidator"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579ECDSAValidator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579ECDSAValidator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579ECDSAValidator.sol";
```

Implementation of an [`ERC7579ECDSA`](#ERC7579ECDSA) as an [`ERC7579Validator`](#ERC7579Validator).

This module provides ECDSA signature validation for ERC-7579 accounts. Each account
can have a single authorized signer (EOA address) that can approve operations through
ECDSA signatures.

Example usage:

```solidity
// Install the validator with a signer address
bytes memory initData = abi.encodePacked(signerAddress);
account.installModule(MODULE_TYPE_VALIDATOR, validatorAddress, initData);
```

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator.sol";
```

Implementation of [`ERC7579Validator`](#ERC7579Validator) that uses [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential)
for confidential multisignature validation of user operations (see ERC-4337).

This extension bypasses the explicit [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.discloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes-) call
and performs disclosure implicitly inside `_rawERC7579Validation`: when the user op
signature includes a valid FHE decryption proof for the multisig result (relayer address
plus proof after the first 20 bytes), the validator accepts the user op without requiring
a prior call to `discloseOperation`. This reduces the flow to two steps:

1. Call [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.requestDiscloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-requestDiscloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes---euint256---bytes---) to validate the multisig
   and make the encrypted result publicly decryptable.
2. Submit the user operation to the entrypoint with the decryption proof in the signature;
   the entrypoint calls `validateUserOp`, which invokes `_rawERC7579Validation` to verify
   the proof (or that the operation was explicitly disclosed via [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.disclosed`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-disclosed-address-bytes32-)).

<Callout>
The operation is not marked as disclosed in storage when validation succeeds via the
inline proof. That is intentional: the fact that the user op executed implies the
multisig was valid and effectively disclosed, and offchain indexers can infer it by
watching entrypoint events instead of relying on the [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.disclosed`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-disclosed-address-bytes32-) boolean.
</Callout>

<Callout>
By definition, the user operation is public in the bundler mempool the moment it's
submitted. Users must not assume that the user operation is confidential, and should consider
front running risks associated with the user operation payload.
</Callout>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_rawERC7579Validation(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigConfidentialValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates the operation by either verifying an inline FHE decryption proof
in the signature (relayer address in the first 20 bytes, proof thereafter) or by
checking [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.disclosed`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-disclosed-address-bytes32-). Does not call [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.discloseOperation`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-discloseOperation-address-bytes32-bytes-)
or set the disclosed flag when the proof is valid; disclosure is implicit in execution.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_rawERC7579Validation(address account, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Returns whether the number of valid signatures meets or exceeds the
threshold set for the target account.

See [`Multisig.tryDecodeMultisignatureCalldata`](/accounts/api/utils#Multisig-tryDecodeMultisignatureCalldata-bytes-) for the encoding details.

Where `signingSigners` are the authorized signers and signatures are their corresponding
signatures of the operation `hash`.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
Besides the standard signer and threshold configuration, this can also include
encrypted signer weights.

See [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the `initData` encoding details.

<Callout>
An account can only call `onInstall` once. If called directly by the account,
the signers, weights, and threshold will be set to the provided data. Future
installations will revert with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* The `signers` array must be non-empty. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigEmptySigners--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.
* Those of [`ERC7579Multisig.addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-addSigners-bytes---) and [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.setSignerWeights`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-setSignerWeights-bytes---uint64---) for signer and weight configuration requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Clears all signers, weights, and total weights.

See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.onUninstall`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-onUninstall-bytes-).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_addSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, euint256[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_addSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to add weight tracking and restrict signer count. See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._addSigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_addSigners-address-euint256---).
Each new signer has a default weight of 1.

Requirements:

* The number of signers must not exceed `type(uint16).max`. Reverts with [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential.ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-ERC7579MultisigTooManySigners--) if exceeded.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_removeSigners-address-euint256---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, euint256[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_removeSigners-address-euint256---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to handle weight tracking during removal. See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._removeSigners`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_removeSigners-address-euint256---).

Cleans up weights for removed signers and updates the total extra weight accordingly.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_maxThreshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_maxThreshold(address account) → euint32</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_maxThreshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to return the maximum threshold based on total weight instead of signer count.
See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._maxThreshold`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_maxThreshold-address-).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_signerWeight-address-euint256-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_signerWeight(address account, euint256 signer) → euint16</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedConfidentialValidator-_signerWeight-address-euint256-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to return the encrypted weight of a signer instead of a fixed weight of 1.
See [`ERC7579MultisigConfidential._signerWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigConfidential-_signerWeight-address-euint256-).

Returns 0 if the signer is not authorized, otherwise returns 1 plus the signer's extra weight.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator.sol">

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator.sol";
```

Extension of [`ERC7579MultisigValidator`](#ERC7579MultisigValidator) that uses [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted) for multisignature validation
with weighted signatures.

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-onInstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onInstall(bytes initData)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-onInstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Sets up the module's initial configuration when installed by an account.
Besides the standard delay and signer configuration, this can also include
signer weights.

The initData should be encoded as:
`abi.encode(bytes[] signers, uint64 threshold, uint64[] weights)`

<Callout>
An account can only call onInstall once. If called directly by the account,
the signer will be set to the provided data. Future installations will revert with
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--).
</Callout>

<Callout>
This function skips `super.onInstall` to avoid setting the threshold during installation
since it can be greater than the total amount of signers. This function adds the signers, sets the weights,
and sets the threshold.
</Callout>

Requirements:

* The `account` must have no signers installed. Reverts with [`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7579MultisigAlreadyInstalled--) if not.
* Those of [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted._decodeMultisigWeightedInitData`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-_decodeMultisigWeightedInitData-bytes-) for the decoding requirements.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-onUninstall-bytes-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">onUninstall(bytes data)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-onUninstall-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Cleans up module's configuration when uninstalled from an account.
Clears all signers, weights, and total weights.

See [`ERC7579Multisig.onUninstall`](#ERC7579Multisig-onUninstall-bytes-).

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_addSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_addSigners(address account, bytes[] newSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_addSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to add weight tracking. See [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---).
Each new signer has a default weight of 1.

In cases where [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) is almost `type(uint64).max` (due to a large `_totalExtraWeight`), adding new
signers could cause the [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) computation to overflow. Adding a [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.totalWeight`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-totalWeight-address-) call after the new
signers are added ensures no such overflow happens.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_removeSigners-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_removeSigners(address account, bytes[] oldSigners)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_removeSigners-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to handle weight tracking during removal. See [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---).

Just like [`ERC7579Multisig._addSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_addSigners-address-bytes---), this function does not emit [`ERC7579MultisigWeighted.ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged`](#ERC7579MultisigWeighted-ERC7579MultisigWeightChanged-address-bytes-uint64-) events. The
[`ERC7579Multisig.ERC7913SignerRemoved`](#ERC7579Multisig-ERC7913SignerRemoved-address-bytes-) event emitted by [`ERC7579Multisig._removeSigners`](#ERC7579Multisig-_removeSigners-address-bytes---) is enough to track weights here.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_validateReachableThreshold-address-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateReachableThreshold(address account)</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_validateReachableThreshold-address-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Override to validate threshold against total weight instead of signer count.

<Callout>
This function intentionally does not call `super._validateReachableThreshold` because the base implementation
assumes each signer has a weight of 1, which is a subset of this weighted implementation. Consider that multiple
implementations of this function may exist in the contract, so important side effects may be missed
depending on the linearization order.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_validateThreshold-address-bytes---"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateThreshold(address account, bytes[] validatingSigners) → bool</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579MultisigWeightedValidator-_validateThreshold-address-bytes---">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

Validates that the total weight of signers meets the [`ERC7579Multisig.threshold`](#ERC7579Multisig-threshold-address-) requirement.
Overrides the base implementation to use weights instead of count.

<Callout>
This function intentionally does not call `super._validateThreshold` because the base implementation
assumes each signer has a weight of 1, which is incompatible with this weighted implementation.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

## `ERC7579P256Validator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579P256Validator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579P256Validator.sol";
```

Implementation of an [`ERC7579P256`](#ERC7579P256) as an [`ERC7579Validator`](#ERC7579Validator).

This module provides P256 signature validation for ERC-7579 accounts. Each account
can have a single authorized P256 signer (public key coordinates) that can approve
operations through P256 signatures.

Example usage:

```solidity
// Install the validator with P256 public key coordinates
bytes memory initData = abi.encodePacked(qx, qy);
account.installModule(MODULE_TYPE_VALIDATOR, validatorAddress, initData);
```

## `ERC7579SessionKeysValidator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579SessionKeysValidator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579SessionKeysValidator.sol";
```

Implementation of [`ERC7579SessionKeys`](#ERC7579SessionKeys) as an [`ERC7579Validator`](#ERC7579Validator).

This module provides session key validation for ERC-7579 accounts. Each account can have
multiple registered ERC-7913 session keys, each with a configurable validity range. A
session key is only authorized to validate operations within its `from` to `until` timepoints.

<Callout type="warn">
By default, any registered key within its validity range can validate any user
operation and sign any ERC-1271 message on behalf of the account. Derived contracts
should restrict the scope of what a session key is authorized to do.
</Callout>

Example usage:

```solidity
// Install the validator with session keys
bytes memory initData = abi.encode(keys, starts, ends);
account.installModule(MODULE_TYPE_VALIDATOR, validatorAddress, initData);
```

<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">isValidSignatureWithSender(address sender, bytes32 hash, bytes signature) → bytes4</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">public</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeysValidator-isValidSignatureWithSender-address-bytes32-bytes-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

See `IERC7579Validator-isValidSignatureWithSender`.

Enforces the validity range of the session key manually since the EntryPoint is not
involved in ERC-1271 signature validation. See [`ERC7579SessionKeys._tryDecodeSignature`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-) for the encoding details.

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579SessionKeysValidator-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-"></a>

<div className="border rounded-md mb-4">
<div className="bg-secondary flex w-full justify-between px-4">
<p className="font-bold text-sm font-mono">_validateUserOp(struct PackedUserOperation userOp, bytes32 userOpHash) → uint256</p>
<div className="flex flex-row items-center gap-2">
<p className="font-light text-sm">internal</p>
<a className="peer" data-card href="#ERC7579SessionKeysValidator-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-">#</a>
</div>
</div>
<div className="px-4">

See [`ERC7579Validator._validateUserOp`](#ERC7579Validator-_validateUserOp-struct-PackedUserOperation-bytes32-).

Decodes the session key from `userOp.signature` and reads its validity range. The range
is packed into the returned `validationData` so the EntryPoint enforces it without
requiring an explicit timestamp check in the validator.
See [`ERC7579SessionKeys._tryDecodeSignature`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-_tryDecodeSignature-bytes-) for the signature encoding details.

<Callout>
Returns `ERC4337Utils.SIG_VALIDATION_FAILED` immediately if [`ERC7579SessionKeys.CLOCK_MODE`](#ERC7579SessionKeys-CLOCK_MODE--) returns a mode
other than timestamp or blocknumber from the same chain (default), as the EntryPoint does not support
other validity range formats.
</Callout>

</div>
</div>

<a id="ERC7579Validator"></a>

<div style={{marginTop: "4em"}} className="w-full flex flex-row items-center justify-between">

## `ERC7579Validator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579Validator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>
</a>

</div>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579Validator.sol";
```

Abstract validator module for ERC-7579 accounts.

This contract provides the base implementation for signature validation in ERC-7579 accounts.
Developers must implement the [`ERC7579ECDSA.onInstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onInstall-bytes-), [`ERC7579ECDSA.onUninstall`](#ERC7579ECDSA-onUninstall-bytes-), and [`ERC7579ECDSAValidator._rawERC7579Validation`](#ERC7579ECDSAValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-)
functions in derived contracts to define the specific signature validation logic.

Example usage:

```solidity
contract MyValidatorModule is ERC7579Validator {
    function onInstall(bytes calldata data) public {
        // Install logic here
    }

    function onUninstall(bytes calldata data) public {
        // Uninstall logic here
    }

    function _rawERC7579Validation(
        address account,
        bytes32 hash,
        bytes calldata signature
    ) internal view override returns (bool) {
        // Signature validation logic here
    }
}
```

Developers can restrict other operations by using the internal [`ERC7579ECDSAValidator._rawERC7579Validation`](#ERC7579ECDSAValidator-_rawERC7579Validation-address-bytes32-bytes-).
Example usage:

```solidity
function execute(
    address account,
    Mode mode,
    bytes calldata executionCalldata,
    bytes32 salt,
    bytes calldata signature
) public virtual {
    require(_rawERC7579Validation(account, hash, signature));
    // ... rest of execute logic
}
```

## `ERC7579WebAuthnValidator` 

<a target="_blank" style={{marginTop: "1.5em"}} href="https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-accounts/blob/central-docs-markdown/packages/contracts/src/modules/validators/ERC7579WebAuthnValidator.sol">
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-github-icon lucide-github"><path d="M15 22v-4a4.8 4.8 0 0 0-1-3.5c3 0 6-2 6-5.5.08-1.25-.27-2.48-1-3.5.28-1.15.28-2.35 0-3.5 0 0-1 0-3 1.5-2.64-.5-5.36-.5-8 0C6 2 5 2 5 2c-.3 1.15-.3 2.35 0 3.5A5.403 5.403 0 0 0 4 9c0 3.5 3 5.5 6 5.5-.39.49-.68 1.05-.85 1.65-.17.6-.22 1.23-.15 1.85v4"/><path d="M9 18c-4.51 2-5-2-7-2"/></svg>

```solidity
import "@openzeppelin-accounts/contracts/modules/validators/ERC7579WebAuthnValidator.sol";
```

Implementation of an [`ERC7579WebAuthn`](#ERC7579WebAuthn) as an [`ERC7579P256Validator`](#ERC7579P256Validator).

This module provides WebAuthn signature validation for ERC-7579 accounts. Each account
can have a single authorized WebAuthn credential (P256 public key) that can approve
operations through WebAuthn authentication assertions.

Example usage:

```solidity
// Install the validator with WebAuthn public key coordinates
bytes memory initData = abi.encodePacked(qx, qy);
account.installModule(MODULE_TYPE_VALIDATOR, validatorAddress, initData);
```
